NereidInference — Third-Party Go Attribution and License Notices

This file covers every module explicitly required by go.mod at its locked
version. The Linux amd64 dependency graphs contain 110 modules in the
default build, 114 with include_nats_messaging, and 49 with no_openziti.
This file is a conservative superset; not every module is linked into
every binary or target operating system.

License identifiers and accompanying texts were checked against the files
shipped in each exact upstream module version. Source links are
version-pinned. The three linked package-local license overrides and the
Go standard library notices are included. The project NOTICE covers
directly adapted source files. Only trailing whitespace is normalized
in the reproduced texts; hashes identify the original upstream files.

This file does not inventory Alpine packages, FFmpeg, or other native
runtime components. Those require a separate per-image, per-architecture
notice and corresponding-source bundle.

For github.com/eclipse/paho.mqtt.golang, this project elects the
BSD-3-Clause alternative permitted by the upstream NOTICE.md.

Exact-version source archives for MPL-2.0 modules in the go.mod inventory:
  https://proxy.golang.org/github.com/hashicorp/errwrap/@v/v1.1.0.zip
  https://proxy.golang.org/github.com/hashicorp/go-multierror/@v/v1.1.1.zip
  https://proxy.golang.org/github.com/shoenig/go-m1cpu/@v/v0.1.6.zip
